# UberBond founder-visible day-by-day activity atlas | 2026-07-14 to 2026-10-09

**Scope:** all 1,156 pull requests (full original description in PRS_*.jsonl), 191 genuine issues (full original issue body in ISSUES_*.jsonl) and 6,228 main commits (all exact commit messages in MAIN_*.jsonl and date-level lists in DAILY_*.md). GitHub created/merged/committed dates are UTC. A PR opening, merged PR, issue or commit is a SOURCE EVENT; it is not independent proof of deployment, test pass, feature use, economic effect or customer. Branch-only commits and issue/PR comments are linked through their original URLs but not line-by-line absorbed into this activity table. The initial repo predates most surviving PR histories.
